<skill_content name="web-perf">
# Skill: web-perf

# Web Performance Audit

Your knowledge of web performance metrics, thresholds, and tooling APIs may be outdated. **Prefer retrieval over pre-training** when citing specific numbers or recommendations.

## Retrieval Sources

| Source | How to retrieve | Use for |
|--------|----------------|---------|
| web.dev | `https://web.dev/articles/vitals` | Core Web Vitals thresholds, definitions |
| Chrome DevTools docs | `https://developer.chrome.com/docs/devtools/performance` | Tooling APIs, trace analysis |
| Lighthouse scoring | `https://developer.chrome.com/docs/lighthouse/performance/performance-scoring` | Score weights, metric thresholds |

## FIRST: Verify MCP Tools Available

Discover available browser and performance tools before starting. Use the capabilities available for the requested audit. If trace tools are unavailable, continue any useful source or network analysis and state which measurements could not be collected.

If the user wants Chrome DevTools MCP setup, consult its [installation guide](https://github.com/ChromeDevTools/chrome-devtools-mcp#quick-start) and use the latest package version. Only change MCP configuration when setup is within the user's authorized scope; otherwise ask first. For clients using `command` and `args`, an example server entry is:

```json
"chrome-devtools": {
  "command": "npx",
  "args": ["-y", "chrome-devtools-mcp@latest"]
}
```

## Key Guidelines

- **Be assertive**: Verify claims by checking network requests, DOM, or codebase—then state findings definitively.
- **Verify before recommending**: Confirm something is unused before suggesting removal.
- **Quantify impact**: Use estimated savings from insights. Don't prioritize changes with 0ms impact.
- **Skip non-issues**: If render-blocking resources have 0ms estimated impact, note but don't recommend action.
- **Be specific**: Say "compress hero.png (450KB) to WebP" not "optimize images".
- **Prioritize ruthlessly**: A site with 200ms LCP and 0 CLS is already excellent—say so.

## Quick Reference

| Task | Tool Call |
|------|-----------|
| Load page | `navigate_page(url: "...")` |
| Start trace | `performance_start_trace(autoStop: true, reload: true)` |
| Analyze insight | `performance_analyze_insight(insightSetId: "...", insightName: "...")` |
| List requests | `list_network_requests(resourceTypes: ["Script", "Stylesheet", ...])` |
| Request details | `get_network_request(reqid: <id>)` |
| A11y snapshot | `take_snapshot(verbose: true)` |

## Workflow

Copy this checklist to track progress:

```
Audit Progress:
- [ ] Phase 1: Performance trace (navigate + record)
- [ ] Phase 2: Core Web Vitals analysis (includes CLS culprits)
- [ ] Phase 3: Network analysis
- [ ] Phase 4: Accessibility snapshot
- [ ] Phase 5: Codebase analysis (skip if third-party site)
```

### Phase 1: Performance Trace

1. Navigate to the target URL:
   ```
   navigate_page(url: "<target-url>")
   ```

2. Start a performance trace with reload to capture cold-load metrics:
   ```
   performance_start_trace(autoStop: true, reload: true)
   ```

3. Wait for trace completion, then retrieve results.

**Troubleshooting:**
- If trace returns empty or fails, verify the page loaded correctly with `navigate_page` first
- If insight names don't match, inspect the trace response to list available insights

### Phase 2: Core Web Vitals Analysis

Use `performance_analyze_insight` to extract key metrics.

**Note:** Insight names may vary across Chrome DevTools versions. If an insight name doesn't work, check the `insightSetId` from the trace response to discover available insights.

Common insight names:

| Metric | Insight Name | What to Look For |
|--------|--------------|------------------|
| LCP | `LCPBreakdown` | Time to largest contentful paint; breakdown of TTFB, resource load, render delay |
| CLS | `CLSCulprits` | Elements causing layout shifts (images without dimensions, injected content, font swaps) |
| Render Blocking | `RenderBlocking` | CSS/JS blocking first paint |
| Document Latency | `DocumentLatency` | Server response time issues |
| Network Dependencies | `NetworkRequestsDepGraph` | Request chains delaying critical resources |

Example:
```
performance_analyze_insight(insightSetId: "<id-from-trace>", insightName: "LCPBreakdown")
```

**Key thresholds (good/needs-improvement/poor):**
- TTFB: < 800ms / < 1.8s / > 1.8s
- FCP: < 1.8s / < 3s / > 3s
- LCP: < 2.5s / < 4s / > 4s
- INP: < 200ms / < 500ms / > 500ms
- TBT: < 200ms / < 600ms / > 600ms
- CLS: < 0.1 / < 0.25 / > 0.25
- Speed Index: < 3.4s / < 5.8s / > 5.8s

### Phase 3: Network Analysis

List all network requests to identify optimization opportunities:
```
list_network_requests(resourceTypes: ["Script", "Stylesheet", "Document", "Font", "Image"])
```

**Look for:**

1. **Render-blocking resources**: JS/CSS in `<head>` without `async`/`defer`/`media` attributes
2. **Network chains**: Resources discovered late because they depend on other resources loading first (e.g., CSS imports, JS-loaded fonts)
3. **Missing preloads**: Critical resources (fonts, hero images, key scripts) not preloaded
4. **Caching issues**: Missing or weak `Cache-Control`, `ETag`, or `Last-Modified` headers
5. **Large payloads**: Uncompressed or oversized JS/CSS bundles
6. **Unused preconnects**: If flagged, verify by checking if ANY requests went to that origin. If zero requests, it's definitively unused—recommend removal. If requests exist but loaded late, the preconnect may still be valuable.

For detailed request info:
```
get_network_request(reqid: <id>)
```

### Phase 4: Accessibility Snapshot

Take an accessibility tree snapshot:
```
take_snapshot(verbose: true)
```

**Flag high-level gaps:**
- Missing or duplicate ARIA IDs
- Elements with poor contrast ratios (check against WCAG AA: 4.5:1 for normal text, 3:1 for large text)
- Focus traps or missing focus indicators
- Interactive elements without accessible names

## Phase 5: Codebase Analysis

**Skip if auditing a third-party site without codebase access.**

Analyze the codebase to understand where improvements can be made.

### Detect Framework & Bundler

Search for configuration files to identify the stack:

| Tool | Config Files |
|------|--------------|
| Webpack | `webpack.config.js`, `webpack.*.js` |
| Vite | `vite.config.js`, `vite.config.ts` |
| Rollup | `rollup.config.js`, `rollup.config.mjs` |
| esbuild | `esbuild.config.js`, build scripts with `esbuild` |
| Parcel | `.parcelrc`, `package.json` (parcel field) |
| Next.js | `next.config.js`, `next.config.mjs` |
| Nuxt | `nuxt.config.js`, `nuxt.config.ts` |
| SvelteKit | `svelte.config.js` |
| Astro | `astro.config.mjs` |

Also check `package.json` for framework dependencies and build scripts.

### Tree-Shaking & Dead Code

- **Webpack**: Check for `mode: 'production'`, `sideEffects` in package.json, `usedExports` optimization
- **Vite/Rollup**: Tree-shaking enabled by default; check for `treeshake` options
- **Look for**: Barrel files (`index.js` re-exports), large utility libraries imported wholesale (lodash, moment)

### Unused JS/CSS

- Check for CSS-in-JS vs. static CSS extraction
- Look for PurgeCSS/UnCSS configuration (Tailwind's `content` config)
- Identify dynamic imports vs. eager loading

### Polyfills

- Check for `@babel/preset-env` targets and `useBuiltIns` setting
- Look for `core-js` imports (often oversized)
- Check `browserslist` config for overly broad targeting

### Compression & Minification

- Check for `terser`, `esbuild`, or `swc` minification
- Look for gzip/brotli compression in build output or server config
- Check for source maps in production builds (should be external or disabled)

## Output Format

Present findings as:

1. **Core Web Vitals Summary** - Table with metric, value, and rating (good/needs-improvement/poor)
2. **Top Issues** - Prioritized list of problems with estimated impact (high/medium/low)
3. **Recommendations** - Specific, actionable fixes with code snippets or config changes
4. **Codebase Findings** - Framework/bundler detected, optimization opportunities (omit if no codebase access)

## Assistant

_Thinking:_

Big findings already:
- 4 render-blocking CSS files: local fonts.css, Google Fonts Rubik CSS, Google Material Symbols CSS, and Base CSS. All in `<head>`, none with media/async. Google Fonts slows FCP/LCP due to 3rd party connection (though preconnect present).
- 22 local woff2 files (Inter, JetBrains Mono, Libre Caslon Text + Rubik? maybe more). The homepage uses only Rubik, Libre Caslon, Material Symbols. Inter and JetBrains Mono are defined but maybe unused; still the CSS is small. However, the browser only downloads used faces (by font-family references). Let's see if Inter/JetBrains referenced anywhere in compiled CSS. grep dist CSS.
- Images: remote Wikimedia originals with no width/height, no `srcset`, no `sizes`; full-resolution images (can be multiple MB) delivered from Wikimedia, causing huge LCP and CLS. The homepage has 30+ images; even lazy-loaded, the hero + above-fold ones load. And reusing the same image multiple times (Trident Pier used twice, Unitree twice, UN hall twice) without cache? They'd be cached by browser.
- No `preload` for LCP image; the LCP image likely the lead hero image which lacks fetchpriority? The 3rd image has fetchpriority high, but which is lead? Let's inspect hero markup order. In the images list, the third image has fetchpriority high. Might be the lead? Need to see.
- CSS: Base CSS 31KB; fine.
- DOM: 85KB HTML, probably ~1000 nodes? Need to measure.
- Fonts: Google's Rubik subsets many (it will download several woff2 for Cyrillic + Latin), each ~15-30KB, plus Material Symbols (variable icon font can be ~300KB). Material Symbols is used for only 5 icons. Big cost.
- Third-party fonts also add connection/TLS and block CSS. Preconnect helps but still.
- The inline theme script is tiny, no layout issue.
- No client JS/hydration on homepage, so "sluggish UI" is likely initial load/scroll (images), not interaction (INP). Could also be CSS `transition-transform` on hover and `group-hover:scale-105` on many images (compositor, cheap).
- Maybe `content-visibility`? no.
- The homepage is 85KB compressed? curl size 85KB (likely uncompressed? curl didn't request gzip). Response maybe gzip. Not important.
- `image.layout: constrained` and `responsiveStyles: true` but since images are remote `<img>` not Astro `<Image>`, no optimization.

Let's dig:
1. Inspect hero section markup to see LCP candidate and dimensions.
2. Check CSS for `Inter`/`JetBrains` usage.
3. Check material symbol font size and Google CSS response.
4. Check a few Wikimedia images Content-Length.
5. Run Lighthouse mobile (and maybe desktop) for metrics.
6. Count DOM nodes via a headless script? Lighthouse gives DOM size.

Let's run Lighthouse. `npx -y lighthouse` may take ~1-2 min. Use `--only-categories=performance --preset=desktop`? For "UI sluggish", mobile is more relevant. Use mobile default. Need `--chrome-flags="--headless=new --no-sandbox"`. Chromium exists at /usr/bin/chromium; set CHROME_PATH.

Run in background? We can run foreground with timeout 300s.

Also get hero markup: grep around "lead" classes. Let's inspect index.astro around hero.

Let's run Lighthouse and image HEAD in parallel.

**Tool: shell**

**Input:**
```json
{
  "command": "cd /tmp && CHROME_PATH=/usr/bin/chromium npx -y lighthouse https://dailybg.org/ --only-categories=performance --output=json --output-path=/tmp/lh-mobile.json --chrome-flags=\"--headless=new --no-sandbox --disable-gpu\" --quiet 2>&1 | tail -5; python3 - <<'EOF'\nimport json\nd=json.load(open('/tmp/lh-mobile.json'))\na=d['audits']\nfor k in ['first-contentful-paint','largest-contentful-paint','total-blocking-time','cumulative-layout-shift','speed-index','interactive','server-response-time','total-byte-weight','dom-size','mainthread-work-breakdown','bootup-time']:\n    x=a.get(k,{})\n    print(f\"{k:32} {x.get('displayValue','-'):>14}  score={x.get('score')}\")\nprint('--- opportunities')\nfor k,x in a.items():\n    if x.get('details',{}).get('type')=='opportunity' and x.get('score') is not None and x.get('score')<0.9:\n        print(k, x.get('displayValue'), x.get('score'))\nEOF",
  "timeout": 420000
}
```

## Assistant

_Thinking:_

Lighthouse: LCP 27.4s! Total byte weight 15MB (!). FCP 1.6s, CLS 0, TBT 10ms, SI 2.0s. So the UI sluggishness is dominated by the enormous image payload (15MB) and LCP 27.4s. Need details: which resource is LCP and size breakdown. Let's parse Lighthouse audits: `largest-contentful-paint-element`, `lcp-breakdown-insight`? Newer LH has `lcp-breakdown-insight` maybe. Also `network-requests` details to see largest images and timings; `resource-summary`. Let's extract.

Also note 15MB total — remote Wikimedia images. 30+ images, each 200KB-2MB. Even lazy loaded, Chrome may load many because they're within viewport/lazy threshold. The /posts page with 198 images would be worse, but focus root.

Also material symbols: Google serves TTF by default to unknown UA (we used android UA but still ttf). Modern UA gets woff2 variable. Need actual browser network. Lighthouse network-requests will tell us actual fonts and sizes.

Let's parse:
- audit 'network-requests' items with resourceType, transferSize, url, startTime/endTime.
- 'largest-contentful-paint-element' / 'lcp-discovery-insight'
- 'resource-summary'
- 'render-blocking-resources', 'uses-responsive-images', 'modern-image-formats', 'offscreen-images', 'unsized-images'
- 'font-display', 'third-party-summary'
- 'mainthread-work-breakdown' detail.

Let's extract.

**Tool: shell**

**Input:**
```json
{
  "command": "cd /tmp && python3 - <<'EOF'\nimport json\nd=json.load(open('/tmp/lh-mobile.json')); a=d['audits']\ndef show(k):\n    x=a.get(k)\n    if not x: print('!! missing',k); return\n    print(f\"\\n### {k}: {x.get('displayValue','')} score={x.get('score')}\")\n    det=x.get('details') or {}\n    if det.get('type')=='table':\n        for it in det.get('items',[])[:15]:\n            print(' ', {kk:vv for kk,vv in it.items() if kk not in ('node',)})\n    elif det.get('type')=='opportunity':\n        print(' ', {kk:vv for kk,vv in det.items() if kk!='items'})\n    elif det.get('type')=='list':\n        for it in det.get('items',[])[:10]: print(' ', it)\nfor k in ['resource-summary','largest-contentful-paint-element','lcp-breakdown-insight','lcp-discovery-insight','render-blocking-resources','unused-css-rules','uses-responsive-images','modern-image-formats','offscreen-images','unsized-images','font-display','third-party-summary','network-dependency-tree-insight','image-delivery-insight']:\n    show(k)\nEOF"
}
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "timeout": 180000
}
```

=== sticky nav
<nav aria-label="Рубрики" class="sticky top-0 z-50 border-b border-border-hairline bg-surface">
	<div
		class="mx-auto flex h-12 max-w-7xl items-center gap-5 overflow-x-auto px-6 font-navigation text-[12px] uppercase tracking-wider lg:px-12"
	>
		{links.map((link) => (
			<a
				href={link.href}
				class:list={[
					"shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap py-1 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary",
					isActive(link) && "border-b-2 border-border-dark font-bold text-primary",
				]}
				aria-current={isActive(link) ? "page" : undefined}
			>
				{link.label}
			</a>
		))}
	</div>
</nav>

Actually, being careful: the diagnosis should be accurate with the numbers I have. Total 15,013 KiB (14.66 MB), images 9 requests 13.41 MB (Lighthouse resource-summary said 14,063,007 = 13.41 MB), fonts 5 requests 1.22 MB, document 14 KB, stylesheets 4 requests 10.4 KB. 99.3% third-party bytes (15,262,218/15,371,731 = 99.3%). 0 scripts. LCP 27.4 s, FCP 1.6 s, TBT 10 ms, CLS 0.

Also note: 28 unique images, 31 img tags, 30 lazy, one fetchpriority high, no width/height.

Important caveat: Lighthouse's 27.4 s LCP is throttled mobile (slow 4G) — that's an extreme, but the relative picture holds. I should mention that clearly and also what a real user on desktop sees.

Let me deliver.

## Root page (`/`) — why it feels sluggish

Short version: it's not JavaScript, and it's not the server. The homepage ships **0 KB of JS** and the document arrives in ~130 ms. It's sluggish because one page pull **~14.7 MB of images and fonts**, almost all of it from third-party origins, and the biggest image is the LCP element and is marked `loading="lazy"`.

Metrics from a throttled mobile Lighthouse run on the live site:

| Metric | Value |
|---|---|
| FCP | 1.6 s |
| **LCP** | **27.4 s** |
| TBT | 10 ms (perfect) |
| CLS | 0 |
| Speed Index | 2.0 s |
| Total payload | **15,013 KiB (14.66 MB)**, 22 requests |
| Scripts | **0 requests, 0 bytes** |

So the page *paints* fast and never blocks the main thread — the score is destroyed entirely by one metric, LCP, which is a network/asset problem.

### 1. The payload is almost all images (91%)

| Type | Requests | Transfer |
|---|---|---|
| Image | 9 | **13.41 MB** |
| Font | 5 | 1.22 MB |
| Document | 1 | 14 KB |
| Stylesheet | 4 | 10.4 KB |
| Script | 0 | 0 |

**99.3% of all bytes are third-party** (15.26 of 15.37 MB). Images come from `upload.wikimedia.org`, `www.aljazeera.com`, `bntnews.bg`, `www.bloombergtv.bg`.

The five that dominate:

| Image | Size | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Brussels NATO HQ | **4.78 MB** | 4334×2435, displayed at 637×525 |
| Sofia University panorama | 2.28 MB | |
| UN General Assembly hall | 1.89 MB | |
| Trident Pier, Gaza | 1.80 MB | |
| Ursula von der Leyen / Zelenskyy | 1.72 MB | **this is the LCP element** |

Across the whole page: **28 unique images** across **31 `<img>` tags**, with **zero `width`/`height` attributes**, **zero `srcset`**, **zero `sizes`**, and no `decoding="async"`. Every image is served at full original resolution and resized by the browser — a 4334-px-wide photo painted into a 637-px box, decoded and held in memory at full size. That's why scrolling feels heavy even though nothing is blocking: the decode work happens on the main thread as images come into view.

Lighthouse estimates **~11.9 MB of avoidable image bytes** — roughly 80% of the image payload.

### 2. The LCP image is explicitly lazy-loaded

The single largest contentful paint element is the Ursula/Zelenskyy Wikimedia image, and its markup has `loading="lazy"` and **no `fetchpriority="high"`**. Lighthouse's `lcp-discovery-insight` flags exactly this. The browser doesn't even *start* fetching the most important image until layout decides it's near the viewport, then waits for a 1.7 MB cross-origin download. On a throttled connection that alone accounts for most of the 27.4 s.

The hero block in `src/pages/index.astro` renders plain `<img src>` with no `srcset`/`sizes`/dimensions, so the browser has no way to pick a smaller variant even though Cloudflare Images is already wired up in the build.

### 3. The font stack costs 1.22 MB for very little

| Font | Size |
|---|---|
| **Material Symbols Outlined** | **1.12 MB** (one variable woff2) |
| Rubik (Google Fonts) | ~51 KB |
| Libre Caslon italic (local) | 28 + 52 KB |

**The Material Symbols variable font is 1.12 MB and the page uses about six icons.** It's loaded render-blocking in `<head>` via `Base.astro`, along with `/fonts/fonts.css`, the Rubik CSS, and the compiled Tailwind CSS — **4 render-blocking stylesheets**. `fonts.css` alone declares 22 `@font-face` rules across Inter, JetBrains Mono and Libre Caslon, so the browser also spends time matching unicode-ranges before it even knows which files to fetch.

### 4. Main-thread cost is layout, not scripting

| Main-thread group | Time |
|---|---|
| Other | 1.44 s |
| **Style & Layout** | **960 ms** |
| Rendering | 228 ms |
| Script evaluation | 41 ms |
| Parse HTML & CSS | 36 ms |

The DOM is 571 elements (35 `<article>`s, 31 images) with 41 CSS `transition` declarations. Style & Layout at ~1 s is the cost of laying out a dense three-column editorial grid with images that have no intrinsic dimensions — the browser can't reserve space, so it has to re-layout as each 4 MB image arrives and reports its real size. Note that CLS still reports 0 because the images are lazy and below the fold during the measured window; scrolling is where you'd feel the reflow.

### What is *not* the problem

- **JS / hydration**: zero script requests, TBT 10 ms, bootup 0 ms. Nothing to fix here.
- **Server / Worker**: TTFB 153 ms, and the edge is serving `cf-cache-status: HIT`.
- **Sticky nav / `backdrop-filter`**: the only `backdrop-filter` hit in the CSS is the global `::backdrop` reset, not an actual blur on the header. 2 occurrences total, no `blur()`, no `will-change`, no `position: fixed`.
- **CSS size**: 43 KB total across all sheets. Irrelevant next to 13 MB of images.

### One-line diagnosis

The homepage is a text-and-CSS page that happens to download 14 MB of full-resolution remote photos plus a 1.1 MB icon font, and it lazy-loads its own LCP image — so first paint is fast and interaction is fine, but the meaningful paint and every scroll step are gated on huge unoptimized image downloads and their decode/layout cost.

The three highest-leverage fixes, when you want them: route hero/list images through Cloudflare Images with `srcset` + explicit dimensions (kills ~12 MB), stop lazy-loading the LCP image and give it `fetchpriority="high"`, and replace the 1.12 MB Material Symbols font with inline SVG for the ~6 icons actually used.

	image: {

=== index.astro
---
import Base from "../layouts/Base.astro";
import { SITE_DESCRIPTION, SITE_LOGO, SITE_TAGLINE, SITE_TITLE, TWITTER_SITE } from "../consts";
import { CATEGORIES } from "../lib/categories";
import { getAllPosts, getFeaturedPosts, toPostSummary } from "../lib/posts-db";

export const prerender = false;

const [allPosts, featuredPosts] = await Promise.all([getAllPosts(), getFeaturedPosts(7)]);

const recent = allPosts.map(toPostSummary);
const curated = featuredPosts.map(toPostSummary);

/**
 * The hero block is driven by editorial `featured` picks, in their configured
 * order. Any unused slots are filled with the newest non-featured stories.
 */
const curatedIds = new Set(curated.map((item) => item.id));
const heroItems = [...curated, ...recent.filter((item) => !curatedIds.has(item.id))].slice(0, 7);

const lead = heroItems[0] ?? null;
const flank = heroItems.slice(1, 3);
const strip = heroItems.slice(3, 7);

// The briefing always shows the most recent stories, independent of curation.
const briefing = recent.slice(0, 4);

/**
 * Build the homepage sections from the canonical category list. Each section
 * keeps a fixed order and shows the latest stories filed under its category.
 */
const sections = CATEGORIES.map((category) => {
	const categoryPosts = recent.filter((item) => item.category?.slug === category.slug);
	return {
		...category,
		count: categoryPosts.length,
		posts: categoryPosts,
	};
});

const navLinks = [
	{ label: "Начало", href: "/", active: true },
	...sections.map((section) => ({ label: section.label, href: `/category/${section.slug}` })),
	{ label: "Архив", href: "/posts" },
	{ label: "Търсене", href: "/search" },
];

const formatDate = (date: Date) =>
	date.toLocaleDateString("bg-BG", { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric" });

const formatShortDate = (date: Date) =>
	date.toLocaleDateString("bg-BG", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", year: "2-digit" });

const formatTime = (date: Date) =>
	date.toLocaleTimeString("bg-BG", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });

const siteUrl = Astro.url.origin;

const jsonLd = {
	"@context": "https://schema.org",
	"@graph": [
		{
			"@type": "WebSite",
			name: SITE_TITLE,
			url: `${siteUrl}/`,
			inLanguage: "bg-BG",
			potentialAction: {
				"@type": "SearchAction",
				target: {
					"@type": "EntryPoint",
					urlTemplate: `${siteUrl}/search?q={search_term_string}`,
				},
				"query-input": "required name=search_term_string",
			},
		},
		{
			"@type": "NewsMediaOrganization",
			name: SITE_TITLE,
			url: `${siteUrl}/`,
			logo: {
				"@type": "ImageObject",
				url: new URL(SITE_LOGO, Astro.site).href,
				width: 512,
				height: 512,
			},
			publishingPrinciples: `${siteUrl}/pages/about/`,
		},
	],
};
---

<Base
	title={`${SITE_TITLE} — ${SITE_TAGLINE}`}
	description={SITE_DESCRIPTION}
	twitterSite={TWITTER_SITE}
	jsonLd={jsonLd}
	navLinks={navLinks}
>
	<style>
		.headline-text a {
			color: inherit;
		}
		.headline-text a:hover {
			color: var(--color-secondary);
		}
	</style>

	{recent.length === 0 ? (
		<section class="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-12 text-center lg:px-12">
			<h1 class="mb-2 font-headline-secondary text-headline-primary">Все още няма публикации</h1>
			<p class="mb-4 text-on-surface-variant">Добавете публикации в D1 базата данни, за да ги видите тук.</p>
		</section>
	) : (
		<div class="w-full bg-background">
			<div class="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-space-sm lg:px-12">
				<div class="flex w-full flex-col">
					<!-- 1. TOP LEAD / HERO COMPLEX (3-column asymmetrical grid) -->
					<section class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-space-md border-b-2 border-border-dark pb-space-md lg:grid-cols-12">
						<!-- LEFT FLANK -->
						<div class="flex flex-col space-y-2 border-border-hairline lg:col-span-3 lg:border-r lg:pr-space-md">
							{flank.map((story) => (
								<article class="group flex flex-col space-y-0.5 border-b border-border-hairline pb-space-sm">
									<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
										<span
											class="font-eyebrow-category text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant"
										>
											{story.tags[0]?.label ?? "Новини"}
										</span>
										<time class="font-timestamp text-timestamp text-outline" datetime={story.date.toISOString()}>
											{formatTime(story.date)}
										</time>
									</div>
									<h3
										class="headline-text font-headline-secondary text-headline-compact leading-snug text-on-surface transition-colors group-hover:text-secondary"
									>
										<a href={story.href}>{story.title}</a>
									</h3>
									<div class="my-1 aspect-[16/10] w-full overflow-hidden bg-surface-subtle">
										{story.image ? (
											<img
												src={story.image}
												alt={story.title}
												class="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105"
												loading="lazy"
											/>
										) : (
											<span class="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-outline">
												img
											</span>
										)}
									</div>
									{story.excerpt && (
										<p class="line-clamp-3 font-body-regular text-[13px] leading-snug text-on-surface-variant">
											{story.excerpt}
										</p>
									)}
								</article>
							))}
						</div>

						<!-- CENTER PRIMARY -->
						<article class="flex flex-col lg:col-span-6 lg:px-space-xs">
							<div class="space-y-1.5">
								<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-space-xs">
									<span
										class="bg-primary px-1.5 py-0.5 font-eyebrow-category text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-on-primary"
									>
										{lead.tags[0]?.label ?? "Избрани"}
									</span>
									<span class="font-timestamp text-timestamp uppercase text-on-surface-variant">
										• {lead.readingTime} МИН ЧЕТЕНЕ • {formatDate(lead.date)}
									</span>
								</div>
								<h1
									class="headline-text font-headline-lead text-[1.75rem] leading-[2.1rem] tracking-tight text-on-surface transition-colors hover:text-secondary md:text-headline-lead"
								>
									<a href={lead.href}>{lead.title}</a>
								</h1>
								<div class="relative my-2 aspect-[16/10] w-full overflow-hidden bg-surface-subtle">
									{lead.image ? (
										<img src={lead.image} alt={lead.title} class="h-full w-full object-cover" fetchpriority="high" />
									) : (
										<span class="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-outline">
											img
										</span>
									)}
									{lead.image && (
										<div
											class="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 flex items-center justify-between bg-ticker-bg/90 px-space-sm py-1 font-timestamp text-[10px] text-surface"
										>
											<span>Снимка: {lead.author ?? "Редакция"}</span>
											<span class="font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-surface-dim">dailyBG</span>
										</div>
									)}
								</div>
								{lead.excerpt && (
									<p class="font-body-lead text-[15px] font-normal leading-snug text-on-surface">
										{lead.excerpt}
									</p>
								)}
								<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-1 font-timestamp text-timestamp">
									{lead.author && <span class="font-bold text-on-surface">{lead.author}</span>}
									<span class="text-outline-variant">•</span>
									<a href={lead.href} class="font-semibold text-secondary hover:underline">
										Прочетете статията →
									</a>
								</div>
							</div>
						</article>

						<!-- RIGHT FLANK: LIVE BRIEFING -->
						<aside class="flex flex-col border-border-hairline lg:col-span-3 lg:border-l lg:pl-space-md">
							<div class="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b-2 border-border-dark pb-1.5">
								<div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
									<span class="relative flex h-2 w-2 shrink-0">
										<span class="absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-live-red opacity-75"></span>
										<span class="relative inline-flex h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-live-red"></span>
									</span>
									<h2 class="whitespace-nowrap font-eyebrow-category text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-primary">
										Брифинг в реално време
									</h2>
								</div>
								<span class="whitespace-nowrap font-timestamp text-[10px] font-semibold text-outline">24/7 поток</span>
							</div>
							<div class="flex flex-col divide-y divide-border-hairline">
								{briefing.map((item, idx) => (
									<article class="group py-1.5">
										<div class="flex items-baseline gap-space-xs">
											<time
												class:list={[
													"shrink-0 font-timestamp text-[11px] font-bold",
													idx === 0 ? "text-live-red" : "text-primary",
												]}
												datetime={item.date.toISOString()}
											>
												{formatTime(item.date)}
											</time>
											<span class="font-eyebrow-category text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-outline-variant">
												{item.tags[0]?.label ?? "Новини"}
											</span>
										</div>
										<h3 class="mt-0.5 font-headline-compact text-[13px] font-semibold leading-snug text-secondary group-hover:underline">
											<a href={item.href}>{item.title}</a>
										</h3>
										{item.excerpt && (
											<p class="mt-0.5 line-clamp-3 font-body-compact text-[11px] leading-snug text-on-surface-variant">
												{item.excerpt}
											</p>
										)}
									</article>
								))}
							</div>
							<a
								href="/posts"
								class="mt-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-1 border border-border-hairline bg-surface-subtle py-1.5 text-center font-eyebrow-category text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-ticker-bg hover:text-surface"
							>
								Към пълния денонощен архив
								<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">arrow_forward</span>
							</a>
						</aside>

						<!-- FOUR-UP DIGEST STRIP -->
						{strip.length > 0 && (
							<div class="mt-1 w-full border-t border-border-hairline pt-space-sm lg:col-span-12">
								<div
									class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-space-md divide-border-hairline md:grid-cols-2 md:divide-x md:divide-y-0 lg:grid-cols-4"
								>
									{strip.map((story, idx) => (
										<article
											class:list={[
												"group flex flex-col justify-between space-y-0.5 md:px-space-sm",
												idx === 0 && "md:pl-0",
												idx === strip.length - 1 && "md:pr-0",
												idx > 0 && "border-t border-border-hairline pt-space-xs md:border-t-0 md:pt-0",
											]}
										>
											<div class="space-y-0.5">
												<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
													<span
														class:list={[
															"font-eyebrow-category text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider",
															idx === 0 ? "text-live-red" : "text-outline",
														]}
													>
														{story.tags[0]?.label ?? "Новини"}
													</span>
													<span class="font-timestamp text-timestamp text-outline">
														{formatShortDate(story.date)}
													</span>
												</div>
												<div class="my-1 aspect-[16/9] w-full overflow-hidden bg-surface-subtle">
													{story.image ? (
														<img
															src={story.image}
															alt={story.title}
															class="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105"
															loading="lazy"
														/>
													) : (
														<span class="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-outline">
															img
														</span>
													)}
												</div>
												<h4
													class="headline-text font-headline-compact text-[13px] font-bold leading-snug text-on-surface transition-colors group-hover:text-secondary group-hover:underline"
												>
													<a href={story.href}>{story.title}</a>
												</h4>
												{story.excerpt && (
													<p class="line-clamp-2 font-body-compact text-[11px] leading-snug text-on-surface-variant">
														{story.excerpt}
													</p>
												)}
											</div>
										</article>
									))}
								</div>
							</div>
						)}
					</section>

					<!-- 2. CATEGORY SECTIONS (generated from post categories) -->
					{sections.map((section) => (
						<section class="border-b-2 border-border-dark py-space-md">
							<div class="mb-space-sm flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-border-hairline pb-1">
								<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-space-sm">
									<h2 class="font-masthead text-headline-secondary uppercase tracking-tight text-primary">
										{section.label}
									</h2>
									<span class="text-border-hairline">/</span>
									<span
										class="font-eyebrow-category text-eyebrow-category uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant"
									>
										{section.count} {section.count === 1 ? "публикация" : "публикации"} в рубриката
									</span>
								</div>
								<a
									href={`/category/${section.slug}`}
									class="flex items-center gap-0.5 font-timestamp text-timestamp text-secondary hover:underline"
								>
									Всички новини от рубриката
									<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">chevron_right</span>
								</a>
							</div>

							{section.posts.length === 1 ? (
								<article class="group flex flex-col gap-space-md md:flex-row">
									<a
										href={section.posts[0].href}
										class="block aspect-[16/10] w-full overflow-hidden bg-surface-subtle md:w-2/5"
									>
										{section.posts[0].image ? (
											<img
												src={section.posts[0].image}
												alt={section.posts[0].title}
												class="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105"
												loading="lazy"
											/>
										) : (
											<span class="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-outline">
												img
											</span>
										)}
									</a>
									<div class="flex flex-1 flex-col justify-between">
										<div class="space-y-1">
											<span class="font-eyebrow-category text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-live-red">
												{section.posts[0].tags.slice(0, 2).map((tag) => tag.label).join(" • ")}
											</span>
											<h4
												class="headline-text font-headline-secondary text-headline-secondary leading-snug text-on-surface transition-colors group-hover:text-secondary"
											>
												<a href={section.posts[0].href}>{section.posts[0].title}</a>
											</h4>
											{section.posts[0].excerpt && (
												<p class="font-body-regular text-[13px] leading-snug text-on-surface-variant">
													{section.posts[0].excerpt}
												</p>
											)}
										</div>
										<div
											class="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-border-hairline pt-1 font-timestamp text-timestamp text-outline"
										>
											<span>{section.posts[0].author ?? "Редакция"}</span>
											<span>{formatTime(section.posts[0].date)}</span>
										</div>
									</div>
								</article>
							) : (
								<div
									class:list={[
										"grid grid-cols-1 gap-space-sm divide-border-hairline md:divide-x",
										section.posts.length >= 4
											? "md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4"
											: section.posts.length === 3
												? "md:grid-cols-3"
												: "md:grid-cols-2",
									]}
								>
									{section.posts.slice(0, 4).map((story, idx, list) => (
										<article
											class:list={[
												"group flex flex-col justify-between pt-space-xs md:pt-0",
												idx === 0 && "md:pr-space-sm",
												idx > 0 && idx < list.length - 1 && "md:px-space-sm",
												idx > 0 && idx === list.length - 1 && "md:pl-space-sm",
												idx > 0 && "border-t border-border-hairline md:border-t-0",
											]}
										>
											<div class="space-y-0.5">
												<div class="my-1 h-[240px] w-full overflow-hidden bg-surface-subtle">
													{story.image ? (
														<img
															src={story.image}
															alt={story.title}
															class="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105"
															loading="lazy"
														/>
													) : (
														<span class="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-outline">
															img
														</span>
													)}
												</div>
												<span
													class:list={[
														"font-eyebrow-category text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider",
														idx === 0 ? "text-live-red" : "text-on-surface-variant",
													]}
												>
													{story.tags.slice(0, 2).map((tag) => tag.label).join(" • ") || "Новини"}
												</span>
												<h4
													class="headline-text font-headline-secondary text-[14px] font-bold leading-snug text-on-surface transition-colors group-hover:text-secondary"
												>
													<a href={story.href}>{story.title}</a>
												</h4>
												{story.excerpt && (
													<p class="line-clamp-3 font-body-compact text-[12px] leading-snug text-on-surface-variant">
														{story.excerpt}
													</p>
												)}
											</div>
											<div
												class="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-border-hairline pt-1 font-timestamp text-timestamp text-outline"
											>
												<span>{story.author ?? "Редакция"}</span>
												<span>{formatTime(story.date)}</span>
											</div>
										</article>
									))}
								</div>
							)}
						</section>
					))}
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>
	)}
</Base>

=== Base.astro
---
import { getCollection } from "astro:content";
import Footer from "../components/Footer.astro";
import Header from "../components/Header.astro";
import { SITE_DESCRIPTION, SITE_IMAGE, SITE_TITLE } from "../consts";
import "../styles/tokens.css";
import "../styles/theme.css";
import "../styles/tailwind.css";

interface Props {
	title: string;
	pageTitle?: string | null;
	description?: string | null;
	image?: string | null;
	imageWidth?: number | null;
	imageHeight?: number | null;
	imageAlt?: string | null;
	canonical?: string | null;
	robots?: string | null;
	type?: "website" | "article";
	publishedTime?: string | null;
	modifiedTime?: string | null;
	author?: string | null;
	articleSection?: string | null;
	articleTags?: string[] | null;
	twitterSite?: string | null;
	twitterImage?: string | null;
	twitterTitle?: string | null;
	twitterDescription?: string | null;
	jsonLd?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
	/** Extra RSS feed advertised for this page (e.g. a category feed). */
	feedUrl?: string | null;
	feedTitle?: string | null;
	/** Hero image to preload for a faster LCP. */
	preloadImage?: string | null;
	/** Homepage chrome: section links used by the footer. */
	navLinks?: Array<{ label: string; href: string; active?: boolean }> | null;
}

const {
	title,
	pageTitle,
	description = SITE_DESCRIPTION,
	image = SITE_IMAGE,
	imageWidth,
	imageHeight,
	imageAlt,
	canonical,
	robots,
	type = "website",
	publishedTime,
	modifiedTime,
	author,
	articleSection,
	articleTags,
	twitterSite,
	twitterImage,
	twitterTitle,
	twitterDescription,
	jsonLd,
	feedUrl,
	feedTitle,
	preloadImage,
	navLinks,
} = Astro.props;

const siteTitle = SITE_TITLE;
// If title already includes site title, use as-is
const fullTitle = title.includes(siteTitle) ? title : `${title} — ${siteTitle}`;

const canonicalURL = canonical ?? new URL(Astro.url.pathname, Astro.site).href;

// Social scrapers require absolute image URLs; resolve relative paths against the site root.
// Pages without their own image fall back to the site-wide default.
const socialImage = image ?? SITE_IMAGE;
const absoluteImage = socialImage ? new URL(socialImage, Astro.site).href : null;
const robotsContent =
	robots ?? "index, follow, max-image-preview:large, max-snippet:-1, max-video-preview:-1";
---

<!doctype html>
<html lang="bg">
	<head>
		<meta charset="UTF-8" />
		<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
		<meta name="generator" content={Astro.generator} />
		<link rel="icon" href="/favicon.ico" />
		<link rel="icon" type="image/png" sizes="16x16" href="/favicon-16x16.png" />
		<link rel="icon" type="image/png" sizes="32x32" href="/favicon-32x32.png" />
		<link rel="apple-touch-icon" sizes="180x180" href="/apple-touch-icon.png" />
		<link rel="manifest" href="/site.webmanifest" />
		<meta name="theme-color" content="#ffffff" />
		<link rel="sitemap" href="/sitemap.xml" />
		<link rel="alternate" type="application/rss+xml" title={siteTitle} href="/rss.xml" />
		<link rel="canonical" href={canonicalURL} />
		<link rel="alternate" hreflang="bg" href={canonicalURL} />
		<link rel="alternate" hreflang="x-default" href={canonicalURL} />
		{feedUrl && (
			<link
				rel="alternate"
				type="application/rss+xml"
				title={`${siteTitle} — ${feedTitle ?? "последни новини"}`}
				href={new URL(feedUrl, Astro.site).href}
			/>
		)}
		{preloadImage && (
			<link rel="preload" as="image" href={new URL(preloadImage, Astro.site).href} fetchpriority="high" />
		)}
		<title>{fullTitle}</title>
		<meta name="description" content={description ?? ""} />
		<meta name="robots" content={robotsContent} />

		<!-- Open Graph / Facebook -->
		<meta property="og:type" content={type} />
		<meta property="og:locale" content="bg_BG" />
		<meta property="og:title" content={pageTitle ?? fullTitle} />
		<meta property="og:description" content={description ?? ""} />
		<meta property="og:url" content={canonicalURL} />
		<meta property="og:site_name" content={siteTitle} />
		{absoluteImage && <meta property="og:image" content={absoluteImage} />}
		{absoluteImage && imageWidth && <meta property="og:image:width" content={String(imageWidth)} />}
		{absoluteImage && imageHeight && <meta property="og:image:height" content={String(imageHeight)} />}
		{absoluteImage && <meta property="og:image:alt" content={imageAlt ?? pageTitle ?? fullTitle} />}

		<!-- Twitter -->
		<meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image" />
		{twitterSite && <meta name="twitter:site" content={twitterSite} />}
		<meta name="twitter:title" content={twitterTitle ?? pageTitle ?? fullTitle} />
		<meta name="twitter:description" content={twitterDescription ?? description ?? ""} />
		{(twitterImage ?? absoluteImage) && <meta name="twitter:image" content={twitterImage ?? absoluteImage} />}

		{/* Article metadata */}
		{publishedTime && <meta property="article:published_time" content={publishedTime} />}
		{modifiedTime && <meta property="article:modified_time" content={modifiedTime} />}
		{author && <meta property="article:author" content={author} />}
		{articleSection && <meta property="article:section" content={articleSection} />}
		{articleTags?.map((tag) => <meta property="article:tag" content={tag} />)}

		{jsonLd && (
			<script type="application/ld+json" set:html={JSON.stringify(jsonLd)} />
		)}

		<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
		<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin />
		<link rel="stylesheet" href="/fonts/fonts.css" />
		<link
			href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Rubik:ital,wght@0,300;0,400;0,500;0,600;0,700;1,400&display=swap"
			rel="stylesheet"
		/>
		<link
			href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Material+Symbols+Outlined:wght,FILL@100..700,0..1&display=swap"
			rel="stylesheet"
		/>
		<script is:inline>
			// Apply an explicit theme choice immediately to prevent flash.
			// With no cookie, color-scheme: light dark follows the OS.
			(function () {
				var c = document.cookie;
				var i = c.indexOf("theme=");
				var theme = i >= 0 ? c.slice(i + 6).split(";")[0] : null;
				if (theme === "dark" || theme === "light") {
					document.documentElement.classList.add(theme);
				}
			})();
		</script>
	</head>
	<body>
		<Header siteTitle={siteTitle} navLinks={navLinks ?? undefined} />

		<main>
			<slot />
		</main>

		<Footer siteTitle={siteTitle} navLinks={navLinks ?? undefined} />

		<style is:global>
			/*
			 * Establish layer order: "base" has lower priority than unlayered
			 * styles, so theme.css overrides always win regardless of source
			 * order in the bundled CSS.
			 */
			@layer base;

			@layer base {
				*:where(:not([class*="emdash"]):not([class*="ec-"])),
				*:where(:not([class*="emdash"]):not([class*="ec-"]))::before,
				*:where(:not([class*="emdash"]):not([class*="ec-"]))::after {
					box-sizing: border-box;
				}

				body,
				h1,
				h2,
				h3,
				h4,
				h5,
				h6,
				p,
				ul,
				ol,
				figure,
				blockquote,
				dl,
				dd {
					margin: 0;
				}

				ul,
				ol {
					padding: 0;
				}

				@supports (color: light-dark(#000, #fff)) {
					:root.light {
						color-scheme: light;
					}

					:root.dark {
						color-scheme: dark;
					}
				}

				html {
					scroll-behavior: smooth;
				}

				body {
					font-family: var(--font-body);
					font-size: var(--font-size-base);
					line-height: var(--leading-relaxed);
					color: var(--color-text);
					background: var(--color-bg);
					-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
					-moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
					min-height: 100vh;
				}

				a:where(:not([class*="emdash"]):not([class*="ec-"])) {
					color: var(--color-brand);
					text-decoration: none;
					transition: color var(--transition-fast);
				}

				a:where(:not([class*="emdash"]):not([class*="ec-"])):hover {
					color: var(--color-brand-hover);
				}

				img {
					max-width: 100%;
					height: auto;
					display: block;
				}

				h1,
				h2,
				h3,
				h4,
				h5,
				h6 {
					font-family: var(--font-heading);
					line-height: var(--leading-tight);
					font-weight: var(--font-weight-heading);
					letter-spacing: var(--tracking-snug);
				}

				h1 {
					font-weight: var(--font-weight-display);
					letter-spacing: var(--tracking-tight);
				}

				::selection {
					background: var(--color-brand);
					color: var(--color-on-brand);
				}
			}
		</style>
	</body>
</html>
=== Header
---
import { CATEGORIES } from "../lib/categories";

interface NavItem {
	label: string;
	href: string;
	active?: boolean;
}

interface Props {
	siteTitle: string;
	navLinks?: NavItem[];
}

const { siteTitle, navLinks } = Astro.props;

const links: NavItem[] =
	navLinks && navLinks.length > 0
		? navLinks
		: [
				{ label: "Начало", href: "/" },
				...CATEGORIES.map((category) => ({
					label: category.label,
					href: `/category/${category.slug}`,
				})),
				{ label: "Архив", href: "/posts" },
				{ label: "Търсене", href: "/search" },
			];

const currentPath = Astro.url.pathname;

const isActive = (link: NavItem) =>
	link.active ??
	(link.href === "/" ? currentPath === "/" : currentPath.startsWith(link.href));

const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString("bg-BG", {
	day: "numeric",
	month: "long",
	year: "numeric",
});
---

<header class="w-full border-b border-border-hairline bg-surface">
	<!-- Utility strip -->
	<div class="border-b border-border-hairline bg-surface-subtle">
		<div class="mx-auto flex h-8 max-w-7xl items-center justify-end px-6 lg:px-12">
			<div class="flex items-center gap-space-lg font-timestamp text-timestamp">
				<a class="text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href="/pages/about/">За нас</a>
				<a class="text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href="/pages/contact/">Контакти</a>
				<a class="text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href="/posts">Архив</a>
				<a class="hidden text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary sm:inline" href="/rss.xml">Бюлетин</a>
				<a class="flex items-center gap-0.5 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href="/search">
					<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">search</span>
					Търсене
				</a>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>

	<!-- Masthead -->
	<div class="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl flex-col gap-2 px-6 py-2 md:flex-row md:items-end md:justify-between lg:px-12">
		<div class="flex flex-col">
			<div class="flex items-baseline gap-space-sm">
				<a
					href="/"
					class="font-masthead text-headline-lead uppercase leading-none tracking-tighter text-primary hover:opacity-90 md:text-masthead"
				>
					{siteTitle}
				</a>
				<span class="mb-1 inline-block h-2.5 w-2.5 bg-live-red"></span>
			</div>
			<p class="mt-0.5 font-editorial-quote text-body-compact italic text-on-surface-variant">
				Независима европейска журналистика и задълбочени геополитически анализи
			</p>
		</div>
		<div class="hidden shrink-0 flex-col items-end font-timestamp text-timestamp text-on-surface-variant md:flex">
			<span class="uppercase tracking-wider">София</span>
			<span class="font-semibold text-primary">{today}</span>
		</div>
	</div>
</header>

<!-- Sticky rubric bar -->
<nav aria-label="Рубрики" class="sticky top-0 z-50 border-b border-border-hairline bg-surface">
	<div
		class="mx-auto flex h-12 max-w-7xl items-center gap-5 overflow-x-auto px-6 font-navigation text-[12px] uppercase tracking-wider lg:px-12"
	>
		{links.map((link) => (
			<a
				href={link.href}
				class:list={[
					"shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap py-1 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary",
					isActive(link) && "border-b-2 border-border-dark font-bold text-primary",
				]}
				aria-current={isActive(link) ? "page" : undefined}
			>
				{link.label}
			</a>
		))}
	</div>
</nav>

=== Footer
---
interface NavItem {
	label: string;
	href: string;
	active?: boolean;
}

interface Props {
	siteTitle: string;
	navLinks?: NavItem[];
}

const { siteTitle, navLinks } = Astro.props;
const year = new Date().getFullYear();

const rubricLinks =
	navLinks && navLinks.length > 0
		? navLinks.slice(0, 7)
		: [
				{ label: "Начало", href: "/" },
				{ label: "Всички публикации", href: "/posts" },
				{ label: "Търсене", href: "/search" },
				{ label: "RSS емисия", href: "/rss.xml" },
			];
---

<footer class="mt-space-md w-full border-t-2 border-border-dark bg-surface">
	<div class="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-space-md lg:px-12">
		<div
			class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-space-md border-b border-border-hairline pb-space-sm md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5"
		>
			<div class="flex flex-col justify-between pr-0 lg:col-span-2 lg:pr-space-md">
				<div class="space-y-1">
					<div class="flex items-baseline gap-space-xs">
						<span class="font-masthead text-headline-secondary uppercase tracking-tighter text-primary">
							{siteTitle}
						</span>
						<span class="inline-block h-2 w-2 bg-live-red"></span>
					</div>
					<p class="max-w-sm font-editorial-quote text-body-compact italic text-on-surface-variant">
						Свободна преса, неподвластна на партийни или олигархични зависимости. Факти, задълбочени
						разследвания и геополитически контекст.
					</p>
				</div>
				<div class="mt-space-sm space-y-1">
					<span class="block font-eyebrow-category text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-primary">
						Седмичен бюлетин
					</span>
					<p class="text-body-compact text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant">
						Обобщение на ключовите европейски решения и анализи всяка неделя в пощата ви.
					</p>
					<form class="flex items-center gap-space-xs" onsubmit="return false">
						<input
							class="w-full max-w-xs border border-border-hairline bg-surface-subtle px-2.5 py-1 text-body-compact text-[12px] text-on-surface focus:border-border-dark focus:outline-none"
							placeholder="Въведете вашия имейл..."
							type="email"
							aria-label="Имейл за бюлетина"
						/>
						<button
							type="submit"
							class="shrink-0 bg-border-dark px-3 py-1 font-headline-compact text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-on-primary transition-colors hover:bg-on-surface-variant"
						>
							Абонамент
						</button>
					</form>
				</div>
			</div>

			<div>
				<h4
					class="mb-1.5 border-b border-border-dark pb-0.5 font-eyebrow-category text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-primary"
				>
					Рубрики
				</h4>
				<ul class="space-y-1 text-body-compact text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant">
					{rubricLinks.map((link) => (
						<li>
							<a class="text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href={link.href}>{link.label}</a>
						</li>
					))}
				</ul>
			</div>

			<div>
				<h4
					class="mb-1.5 border-b border-border-dark pb-0.5 font-eyebrow-category text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-primary"
				>
					Институция
				</h4>
				<ul class="space-y-1 text-body-compact text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant">
					<li><a class="text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href="/pages/about/">За нас</a></li>
					<li><a class="text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href="/pages/contact/">Контакти</a></li>
					<li><a class="text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href="/pages/careers/">Кариери</a></li>
					<li><a class="text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href="/rss.xml">RSS потоци</a></li>
				</ul>
			</div>

			<div>
				<h4
					class="mb-1.5 border-b border-border-dark pb-0.5 font-eyebrow-category text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-primary"
				>
					Контакт с редакцията
				</h4>
				<div class="space-y-1 text-body-compact text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant">
					<p>Сигнали, обратна връзка и предложения за теми:</p>
					<p class="font-timestamp font-bold text-primary">contact@dailybg.org</p>
					<p class="font-timestamp text-timestamp text-outline">София, България</p>
					<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm pt-0.5 text-primary">
						<a href="/rss.xml" aria-label="RSS" class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary">rss_feed</a>
						<a href="mailto:contact@dailybg.org" aria-label="Имейл" class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary">mail</a>
						<a href="/search" aria-label="Търсене" class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary">search</a>
						<a href="/pages/about/" aria-label="За нас" class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] text-primary">info</a>
					</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		</div>

		<div
			class="flex flex-col items-center justify-between gap-1 pt-2 font-timestamp text-[11px] text-on-surface-variant sm:flex-row"
		>
			<p>© {year} {siteTitle}. Всички права запазени.</p>
			<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm">
				<a class="text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href="/pages/about/">За нас</a>
				<a class="text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href="/pages/contact/">Контакти</a>
				<a class="text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:text-primary" href="/pages/careers/">Кариери</a>
			</div>
		</div>
	</div>
</footer>
=== PostCard
---
import { Image } from "astro:assets";
import type { ImageMetadata } from "astro";

interface Props {
	title: string;
	excerpt?: string;
	/** ImageMetadata for local assets, or an absolute URL string for remote images */
	heroImage?: ImageMetadata | string;
	href: string;
	date?: Date;
	readingTime?: number;
	tags?: Array<{ slug: string; label: string }>;
	author?: string;
}

const { title, excerpt, heroImage, href, date, readingTime, tags, author } = Astro.props;

const formattedDate = date
	? date.toLocaleDateString("bg-BG", {
			year: "numeric",
			month: "short",
			day: "numeric",
		})
	: null;

const imgSrc = typeof heroImage === "string" ? heroImage : heroImage?.src;
---

<article class="post-card">
	<a href={href} class="card-link">
		{imgSrc ? (
			<div class="card-image">
				<img src={imgSrc} alt={title} />
			</div>
		) : (
			<div class="card-placeholder" />
		)}
		<div class="card-body">
			<div class="card-meta">
				{formattedDate && <time class="card-date">{formattedDate}</time>}
				{author && (
					<>
						{formattedDate && <span class="meta-dot" />}
						<span class="card-byline-name">{author}</span>
					</>
				)}
				{readingTime && (
					<>
						<span class="meta-dot" />
						<span>{readingTime} мин</span>
					</>
				)}
			</div>
			<h2 class="card-title">{title}</h2>
			{excerpt && <p class="card-excerpt">{excerpt}</p>}
		</div>
	</a>
	{tags && tags.length > 0 && (
		<div class="card-tags">
			{tags.slice(0, 2).map((tag) => (
				<a href={`/tag/${tag.slug}`} class="card-tag">
					{tag.label}
				</a>
			))}
		</div>
	)}
</article>

<style>
	.post-card {
		display: flex;
		flex-direction: column;
	}

	.card-link {
		display: block;
		text-decoration: none;
		color: inherit;
	}

	.card-image {
		aspect-ratio: 16 / 10;
		overflow: hidden;
		border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
		background: var(--color-surface);
		margin-bottom: var(--spacing-4);
	}

	.card-image img {
		width: 100%;
		height: 100%;
		object-fit: cover;
		transition: transform 0.3s ease;
	}

	.card-link:hover .card-image img {
		transform: scale(1.03);
	}

	.card-placeholder {
		aspect-ratio: 16 / 10;
		border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
		background: var(--color-surface);
		margin-bottom: var(--spacing-4);
	}

	.card-body {
		flex: 1;
	}

	.card-meta {
		display: flex;
		align-items: center;
		flex-wrap: wrap;
		column-gap: var(--spacing-3);
		row-gap: 0;
		font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
		color: var(--color-muted);
		margin-bottom: var(--spacing-2);
		text-transform: uppercase;
		letter-spacing: var(--tracking-wide);
	}

	.card-meta time,
	.card-meta span:not(.meta-dot) {
		white-space: nowrap;
	}

	.meta-dot {
		width: 3px;
		height: 3px;
		border-radius: 50%;
		background: var(--color-muted);
	}

	.card-date {
		color: var(--color-muted);
	}

	.card-byline-name {
		font-weight: 500;
		color: var(--color-text-secondary);
	}

	.card-title {
		font-size: var(--font-size-lg);
		font-weight: var(--font-weight-heading);
		line-height: var(--leading-snug);
		letter-spacing: var(--tracking-snug);
		margin-bottom: var(--spacing-2);
		transition: color var(--transition-fast);
	}

	.card-link:hover .card-title {
		color: var(--color-brand);
	}

	.card-excerpt {
		font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
		line-height: var(--leading-relaxed);
		color: var(--color-text-secondary);
		display: -webkit-box;
		-webkit-line-clamp: 2;
		-webkit-box-orient: vertical;
		overflow: hidden;
	}

	.card-tags {
		display: flex;
		flex-wrap: wrap;
		gap: var(--spacing-2);
		margin-top: var(--spacing-3);
	}

	.card-tag {
		display: inline-block;
		padding: var(--tag-padding-y) var(--spacing-2);

# Pricing

Last updated Oct 19, 2026|Copy as Markdown|[View as Markdown](index.md)|[Agent
setup](/agent-setup/)

OverviewImages FreeImages PaidMetrics Images Transformed Images Stored Images
Delivered

By default, all users are on the Images Free plan. The Free plan includes
access to the transformations feature, which lets you optimize images stored
outside of Images, like in [R2](/r2/).

The Paid plan allows transformations, as well as access to storage in Images.

Pricing is dependent on which features you use. The table below shows which
metrics are used for each use case.

Use case | Metrics | Availability  
---|---|---  
Optimize images stored outside of Images | Images Transformed | Free and Paid plans  
Optimize images that are stored in Cloudflare Images | Images Stored, Images Delivered | Only Paid plans  
  
## Images Free

On the Free plan, you can request up to 5,000 unique transformations each
month for free.

Once you exceed 5,000 unique transformations:

  * Existing transformations in cache will continue to be served as expected.
  * New transformations will return a `9422` error. If your source image is from the same domain where the transformation is served, then you can use the [`onerror` parameter](/images/optimization/features/#onerror) to redirect to the original image.
  * You will not be charged for exceeding the limits in the Free plan.

To request more than 5,000 unique transformations each month, you can purchase
an Images Paid plan.

## Images Paid

When you purchase an Images Paid plan, you can choose your own storage or add
storage in Images.

Metric | Pricing  
---|---  
Images Transformed | First 5,000 unique transformations included + $0.50 / 1,000 unique transformations / month  
Images Stored | $5 / 100,000 images stored / month  
Images Delivered | $1 / 100,000 images delivered / month  
  
If you optimize an image stored outside of Images, then you will be billed
only for Images Transformed.

Images Stored and Images Delivered apply only to images that are stored in
your Images bucket. When you optimize a hosted image through the image
delivery URL, then this counts toward Images Delivered — not Images
Transformed. However, if you optimize a hosted image through the Images
binding, then this counts toward Images Transformed.

## Metrics

### Images Transformed

When using the [Images binding](/images/optimization/binding/) or optimizing
images stored outside of Images, you will incur $0.50 for every 1,000 unique
transformations.

A transformation is a request to optimize an original image based on a set of
[supported parameters](/images/optimization/features/). Billing follows these
rules:

  * Each combination of a source image and optimization parameters counts as a unique transformation. For example, serving `thumbnail.jpg` as 100x100 pixels and 200x200 pixels count as two unique transformations.
  * Cloudflare counts the number of unique transformations requested in each calendar month.
  * Repeated requests for the same transformation within the same calendar month count only once, regardless of how many times the transformed image is served.
  * Cloudflare excludes `format` and `quality` when determining whether a transformation is unique. Requests with different `format` and `quality` settings count as one unique transformation. For example, `width=100,format=auto/thumbnail.jpg` counts as one unique transformation whether Cloudflare serves the image as AVIF or WebP.
  * Calls to the Images binding's [`.info()`](/images/optimization/binding/#infostream) method are not billed.

#### Example #1

If you serve 2,000 remote images in five different sizes each month, then this
results in 10,000 unique transformations. Your estimated cost for the month
would be:

| Usage | Included | Billable quantity | Price  
---|---|---|---|---  
Transformations | 10,000 unique transformations 1 | 5,000 | 5,000 | $2.50 2  
  
#### Example #2

If you use [R2](/r2/) for storage then your estimated monthly costs will be
the sum of your monthly Images costs and monthly [R2
costs](/r2/pricing/#storage-usage).

For example, if you upload 5,000 images to R2 with an average size of 5 MB,
and serve 2,000 of those images in five different sizes, then your estimated
cost for the month would be:

| Usage | Included | Billable quantity | Price  
---|---|---|---|---  
Storage | 25 GB 3 | 10 GB | 15 GB | $0.22 4  
Class A operations | 5,000 writes 5 | 1 million | 0 | $0.00 6  
Class B operations | 10,000 reads 7 | 10 million | 0 | $0.00 8  
Transformations | 10,000 unique transformations 9 | 5,000 | 5,000 | $2.50 10  
**Total** |  |  |  | **$2.72**  
  
### Images Stored

Storage in Images is available only with an Images Paid plan. You can purchase
storage in increments of $5 for every 100,000 images stored per month.

You can create predefined variants to specify how an image should be resized,
such as `thumbnail` as 100x100 and `hero` as 1600x500.

Only uploaded images count toward Images Stored; defining variants will not
impact your storage limit.

### Images Delivered

For images that are stored in Images, you will incur $1 for every 100,000
images delivered per month. This metric does not include transformed images
that are stored in remote sources.

Cloudflare counts an image delivery as a billable request only when the
response has an HTTP `200` status code and is larger than 1,024 bytes.
Responses with another status code or a size of 1,024 bytes or less are not
billed.

#### Example

A retail website has a product page that uses Images to serve 10 images. If
the page was visited 10,000 times this month, then this results in 100,000
images delivered — or $1.00 in billable usage.

## Footnotes

  1. 2,000 original images × 5 sizes ↩

  2. (5,000 transformations / 1,000) × $0.50 ↩

  3. 5,000 objects × 5 MB per object ↩

```astro
---
import { Image } from "astro:assets";
const REMOTE = new Set([...]);
const { src, alt, ratio = "16/10", widths = [400, 800, 1200], sizes, priority = false, class: cls } = Astro.props;
const [w, h] = ratio.split("/").map(Number);
const managed = src && REMOTE.has(new URL(src).hostname);
---
{managed ? (
  <Image src={src} alt={alt} widths={widths} sizes={sizes}
    width={1200} height={Math.round(1200*h/w)} format="avif"
    loading={priority ? "eager" : "lazy"} fetchpriority={priority ? "high" : "auto"}
    decoding="async" class={cls} />
) : (
  <img src={src} alt={alt} loading={priority ? "eager" : "lazy"} decoding="async" class={cls} />
)}
```

Here's what I'd do, in order of impact-per-hour. I verified each option against the actual code and the Cloudflare plan you're on (Images **Free**, which matters for the image pipeline choice).

## Phase 1 — ~1 hour, no new machinery, biggest LCP win

These four changes don't reduce total bytes but they fix *when* the important bytes are discovered, and they kill the 1.12 MB font.

**1.1 Stop lazy-loading the hero.** The LCP element is a `loading="lazy"` image in the hero grid (`src/pages/index.astro`). The lead already has `fetchpriority="high"`, but the flank/strip images — where the LCP image actually lives — are all lazy. Make the whole hero eager, and preload the first one:

```astro
<!-- in index.astro, Base already supports this prop -->
<Base ... preloadImage={lead.image} />
```

```astro
<!-- lead: keep fetchpriority, add decoding -->
<img src={lead.image} ... fetchpriority="high" decoding="async" />
<!-- flank images: drop loading="lazy" -->
<img src={story.image} ... decoding="async" />
```

Keep everything from the category sections down lazy.

**1.2 Replace Material Symbols with inline SVG.** The page uses exactly **6 icons** (`arrow_forward`, `chevron_right` ×2, `search` ×2, `rss_feed`, `mail`, `info`) and downloads a **1,119 KB** variable font for them, plus one render-blocking Google stylesheet. A tiny `src/components/Icon.astro` with a lookup of 6 paths removes 1.12 MB, one blocking request, and one third-party connection. This is the single best bytes-per-effort trade on the page.

**1.3 `decoding="async"` on every image.** Full-resolution JPEGs are currently decoded synchronously as they enter the viewport. This is most of the scroll jank and part of the 1.44 s "Other" main-thread bucket.

**1.4 `content-visibility: auto` on the category sections.** Style & Layout is 960 ms for 571 elements / 35 articles. Adding `content-visibility: auto; contain-intrinsic-size: auto 800px` to each `<section>` in `index.astro` skips layout+paint for the ~25 off-screen cards until they scroll near the viewport. No visual change, no JS.

**Phase 1 expected:** LCP 27.4 s → roughly 4–6 s on throttled mobile (still gated by the 1.7 MB LCP file), payload 14.66 MB → 13.5 MB, one fewer blocking request.

## Phase 2 — the actual payload fix (~half a day)

The 13.4 MB of images has to be resized and re-encoded. You already have the right tool wired up: the build emits an `IMAGES` binding (`dist/server/wrangler.json` → `"images": { "binding": "IMAGES" }`), and `astro.config.mjs` already sets `image: { layout: "constrained", responsiveStyles: true }`. It just can't touch remote images because no `remotePatterns` are declared.

**2.1 Allow the four hosts.** In `astro.config.mjs`:

```js
image: {
  layout: "constrained",
  responsiveStyles: true,
  remotePatterns: [
    { protocol: "https", hostname: "upload.wikimedia.org" },
    { protocol: "https", hostname: "www.aljazeera.com" },
    { protocol: "https", hostname: "bntnews.bg" },
    { protocol: "https", hostname: "www.bloombergtv.bg" },
  ],
},
```

**2.2 One shared image component.** Add `src/components/PostImage.astro` wrapping `astro:assets`'s `<Image>`, with `widths={[400, 800, 1200]}`, a `sizes` string per context, explicit `width`/`height` matching the existing `aspect-[16/10]` / `aspect-[16/9]` boxes, `format="avif"`, `loading`/`fetchpriority` from a `priority` prop, and a plain `<img>` fallback when the URL's host isn't in the allow-list. Then swap the 31 hand-written `<img>` tags (index, PostCard, category, tag, article, search) for it. `widths` makes Astro emit `srcset`, so a phone pulls ~40 KB instead of 4.78 MB.

**2.3 Quota check — this is the thing to watch.** Workers Free includes **5,000 unique transformations/month**. A transformation is unique per (source image + params), and repeated views are free. At ~300 distinct hero images × 3 widths ≈ 900/month, plus article pages — comfortably under. Past 5,000 you get `9422` and *new* sizes fail while cached ones keep serving, so add a quick usage check in the first month. If you'd rather not depend on it, the alternative is ingesting images into R2 and serving pre-generated variants — better, but it means touching the n8n/admin write path.

**2.4 Preload must become responsive.** Once images are `srcset`-based, `Base.astro`'s `preloadImage` (plain `href`) will preload the wrong candidate. Extend it to emit `imagesrcset`/`imagesizes`, or drop the preload and rely on `fetchpriority="high"` on the eager hero image.

**Phase 2 expected:** images 13.41 MB → **~1–1.5 MB**, total page ~2.5 MB, LCP ~2–3 s mobile, and scrolling stops decoding 4-megapixel JPEGs.

## Phase 3 — small cleanups, do whenever

- **Two images are broken right now.** `bntnews.bg` and `bloombergtv.bg` hero URLs return `text/html` (hotlink-blocked), so those cards render a broken image. Worth fixing at the content level.
- **Fonts:** after removing Material Symbols, only Rubik (~50 KB) and Libre Caslon italic (~79 KB) actually load on `/`. `public/fonts/fonts.css` still declares 22 faces for Inter/JetBrains/Libre Caslon that the homepage never uses — harmless bytes, but it's dead config. Self-hosting the two Rubik subsets you use would remove the last two third-party requests and let you `preload` them properly.
- **31 images on one page is a lot** even when optimized. The category sections could use one image each instead of four, which also cuts the DOM from 571 elements.

## What I'd expect overall

| | now | after Phase 1 | after Phase 2 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Payload | 14.66 MB | ~13.5 MB | **~2.5 MB** |
| Images | 13.41 MB / 9 req | 13.41 MB | **~1.2 MB** |
| Fonts | 1.22 MB | **~0.08 MB** | ~0.08 MB |
| LCP (throttled mobile) | 27.4 s | ~4–6 s | **~2–3 s** |
| TBT / CLS | 10 ms / 0 | unchanged | unchanged |

Phase 1 is safe and reversible; Phase 2 is where the real win is but touches every image call site and adds the transformations quota dependency.

Short answer: **no, $0** — and there's no path where it silently bills you. Details, because the image pipeline is the only piece with a metered quota:

## What actually gets metered

| Thing | Free allowance | Your projected usage | Cost |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Images transformations** | 5,000 unique/month | ~600–1,500/month | **$0** |
| Worker requests | 100,000/day | +~9 per page view, mostly browser-cached | **$0** |
| Worker CPU | 10 ms/invocation | unchanged by this work | **$0** |
| Bandwidth / egress | unmetered | drops ~80% | **$0** |
| D1 | unchanged | unchanged | **$0** |
| R2 (only if Phase 3) | 10 GB storage, 10M reads, 1M writes/mo | ~200 images | **$0** |

**The important detail about the 5,000 transformations:** on the Images **Free** plan, exceeding the limit does **not** produce a bill. Cloudflare's docs are explicit — "You will not be charged for exceeding the limits in the Free plan." Past 5,000, *new* size variants return a `9422` error while everything already requested keeps serving from cache. So the failure mode is degraded images, not a surprise invoice.

And the count is more forgiving than it sounds: a "transformation" is unique per **(source image + parameters) per month**, and the docs state format and quality are excluded from uniqueness. Repeated views are free. So:

- ~200 posts × 3 widths = **600/month** if every post image is viewed at every size
- the homepage's 31 images × 3 widths = **93/month**
- worst realistic case with articles, categories and tags: **~1,500/month**

You'd need roughly 1,600+ distinct images before you'd even approach the ceiling. I'd still add a 2-line safety valve in `PostImage.astro` — an inline `onerror` that swaps to the original URL — so that if you ever hit it, readers get a big image instead of a broken one.

## Other cost-related points

- **No new dependencies.** Phase 1 is an SVG component plus markup edits. Phase 2 uses `astro:assets`, which ships with Astro core and is already configured; no `@astrojs/image`, no third-party resizer, no subscription.
- **Transform work doesn't run in your Worker's CPU budget.** It executes in the Images service; the Worker just proxies the stream. Your 10 ms Free-plan limit (the thing that took the site down) is unaffected.
- **Transform responses are already cached for a year**, verified in the built worker: `image-transform-endpoint` sets `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`. The URL includes width + format, so a given size is fetched once per browser. I'd still add `/_image` to `src/middleware.ts`'s Cache API allow-list so the *edge* caches it too and repeat visitors across devices don't re-invoke the Worker. Still free either way.
- **Third parties cost nothing.** Wikimedia/Al Jazeera/BNT are free to hotlink. Worth knowing: you're currently pulling **28 full-resolution Wikimedia files per homepage view**, which is impolite at best and the kind of thing that gets rate-limited; resizing to 400–1200 px is both faster and less likely to be blocked.

## The only scenarios that would cost money

1. If you *chose* to buy Images Paid for headroom — $0.50 per 1,000 unique transformations beyond the first 5,000, i.e. pennies at your volume. **Not needed**, and I wouldn't recommend it until you're consistently near 5,000.
2. If you ever exceed 100,000 Worker requests/day → Workers Paid at $5/month. At your traffic that would require an order-of-magnitude traffic increase, and it's a flat fee, not overage billing.

So: Phase 1 and Phase 2 are both free at your volume, with hard caps rather than metered billing on the only quota that matters. Want me to start on Phase 1?

async function transform(rawUrl, images, assets) {
	const url = new URL(rawUrl);
	const href = url.searchParams.get("href");
	if (!href || isRemotePath(href) && !isRemoteAllowed(href, imageConfig)) return new Response("Forbidden", { status: 403 });

		png: "image/png",
		gif: "image/gif",
		webp: "image/webp",

	if (!outputFormat) return new Response(`Unsupported format: ${url.searchParams.get("f")}`, { status: 400 });

		format: outputFormat

}

```astro
---
import { Image } from "astro:assets";

interface Props {
  src?: string | null;
  alt: string;
  class?: string;
  /** CSS sizes attribute; describes the rendered width. */
  sizes: string;
  /** Intrinsic aspect ratio, e.g. "16/10". */
  ratio?: string;
  widths?: number[];
  priority?: boolean;
  loading?: "lazy" | "eager";
}
const { src, alt, class: className, sizes, ratio = "16/10", widths = [400, 640, 800, 1200], priority = false, loading } = Astro.props;

const [rw, rh] = ratio.split("/").map(Number);
const width = widths[widths.length - 1];
const height = Math.round((width * rh) / rw);

// Only send images we can transform through the image service; otherwise let
// the browser load the original so a new host never breaks a card.
const ALLOWED = new Set(["upload.wikimedia.org", "www.aljazeera.com", "bntnews.bg", "www.bntnews.bg", "bloombergtv.bg", "www.bloombergtv.bg"]);
let managed = false;
try { managed = !!src && ALLOWED.has(new URL(src).hostname); } catch {}

const imgLoading = loading ?? (priority ? "eager" : "lazy");
---
{managed ? (
  <Image
    src={src!}
    alt={alt}
    widths={widths}
    sizes={sizes}
    width={width}
    height={height}
    format="webp"
    layout="none"
    quality={75}
    loading={imgLoading}
    fetchpriority={priority ? "high" : undefined}
    decoding="async"
    class={className}
  />
) : (
  <img src={src ?? undefined} alt={alt} loading={imgLoading} decoding="async" fetchpriority={priority ? "high" : undefined} class={className} />
)}
```

```json
{
  "content": "---\nimport { Image } from \"astro:assets\";\n\n/**\n * Responsive image for remote hero URLs stored in D1.\n *\n * Remote images are proxied through the Cloudflare Images binding by the\n * adapter's `/_image` endpoint, which resizes, re-encodes and edge-caches the\n * result. That turns a 4.7 MB Wikimedia original into a ~60-150 KB WebP.\n *\n * Any host outside `ALLOWED_HOSTS` falls back to a plain `<img>` so adding a\n * new source can never break a card.\n */\nconst ALLOWED_HOSTS = new Set([\n\t\"upload.wikimedia.org\",\n\t\"www.aljazeera.com\",\n\t\"aljazeera.com\",\n\t\"bntnews.bg\",\n\t\"www.bntnews.bg\",\n\t\"bloombergtv.bg\",\n\t\"www.bloombergtv.bg\",\n]);\n\ninterface Props {\n\tsrc?: string | null;\n\talt: string;\n\t/** Rendered widths, e.g. `(min-width: 1024px) 640px, 100vw`. */\n\tsizes: string;\n\t/** Candidate widths, smallest first. Keep this list short to save transforms. */\n\twidths?: number[];\n\t/** Intrinsic aspect ratio, used for the generated width/height attributes. */\n\tratio?: string;\n\tclass?: string;\n\t/** Above-the-fold images: eager + high priority. */\n\tpriority?: boolean;\n}\n\nconst {\n\tsrc,\n\talt,\n\tsizes,\n\twidths = [400, 640, 900],\n\tratio = \"16/10\",\n\tclass: className,\n\tpriority = false,\n} = Astro.props;\n\nconst [ratioW, ratioH] = ratio.split(\"/\").map(Number);\nconst width = widths[widths.length - 1];\nconst height = Math.round((width * ratioH) / ratioW);\n\nlet managed = false;\ntry {\n\tmanaged = !!src && ALLOWED_HOSTS.has(new URL(src).hostname);\n} catch {\n\tmanaged = false;\n}\n\nconst loading = priority ? \"eager\" : \"lazy\";\nconst fetchpriority = priority ? \"high\" : undefined;\n---\n\n{\n\tmanaged ? (\n\t\t<Image\n\t\t\tsrc={src as string}\n\t\t\talt={alt}\n\t\t\twidth={width}\n\t\t\theight={height}\n\t\t\twidths={widths}\n\t\t\tsizes={sizes}\n\t\t\tformat=\"webp\"\n\t\t\tquality={75}\n\t\t\tlayout=\"none\"\n\t\t\tloading={loading}\n\t\t\tfetchpriority={fetchpriority}\n\t\t\tdecoding=\"async\"\n\t\t\tclass={className}\n\t\t/>\n\t) : (\n\t\t<img\n\t\t\tsrc={src ?? undefined}\n\t\t\talt={alt}\n\t\t\twidth={width}\n\t\t\theight={height}\n\t\t\tloading={loading}\n\t\t\tfetchpriority={fetchpriority}\n\t\t\tdecoding=\"async\"\n\t\t\tclass={className}\n\t\t/>\n\t)\n}\n",
  "path": "/home/tht/Projects/dailybg-rewrite/src/components/PostImage.astro"
}
```
